| Calling themselves 'overemployed', some Australians are holding down multiple full-time jobs at once, usually without their employers' knowledge. <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">But</span> working long hours and bending the company rules can come at a cost. | Tự gọi mình là “over-employed”, những người Úc này đang làm việc toàn thời gian cho nhiều nơi cùng một lúc...tất nhiên chủ lao động của họ không hề biết. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Việc này<sup>A</sup></span> làm dấy lên câu hỏi về sự trung thành với công ty trong thời đại làm việc từ xa. |
| It may sound physically impossible, but Ash (not her real name) has worked up to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">seven jobs</span> at the same time — four of which were <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">full-time</span>. | Nghe có vẻ bất khả thi, nhưng Nga (tên đã được thay đổi) từng cùng lúc làm đến <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bảy công việc</span>, trong đó có bốn công việc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">toàn thời gian</span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">You<sup>E</sup></span> know how people have 'f--- you' money? <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> have 'f--- you' <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">jobs</span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she<sup>A</sup></span> told The Feed. |  |
| The Australian <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">software industry worker</span> has been juggling <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple jobs</span> for the past five years, working up to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">20 hours a day</span>, seven days a week. | <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Cô<sup>A</sup></span> làm việc trong ngành phần mềm ở Úc và đã xoay sở <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhiều công việc</span> suốt năm năm qua, có lúc làm đến 20 giờ mỗi ngày, bảy ngày trong tuần. |
| When <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she<sup>A</sup></span> worked <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">five jobs</span> or more, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash</span>'s days were hectic. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> woke up at 4am and immediately checked her emails. After a quick shower, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she<sup>A</sup></span> ate breakfast at her desk and worked her <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">first job</span> until 8.30am. | Khi đảm nhận từ <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">năm công việc</span> trở lên, một ngày của <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga</span> cực kỳ bận rộn. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Cô<sup>A</sup></span> dậy lúc 4 giờ sáng và lập tức kiểm tra email. Sau khi tắm nhanh, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> vừa ăn sáng vừa làm việc tại bàn ăn cho <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công việc đầu tiên</span> đến 8 giờ 30 sáng. |
| <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> then headed into the office for her nine-to-five <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">government role</span>, spending her lunch break working in the lobby or car. | Sau đó, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> vào văn phòng cho <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công việc chính thức</span> từ 9 giờ đến 5 giờ tại một cơ quan chính phủ, tranh thủ giờ nghỉ trưa làm việc trong sảnh hoặc trên xe. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> always had a computer and a dongle, so I'd do my other meetings during <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">lunchtime</span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she said<sup>A</sup></span>. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> lúc nào cũng mang theo máy tính và USB để lên mạng, nên có thể họp nhóm vào <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giờ nghỉ</span>,” <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô kể<sup>A</sup></span>. |
| <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> squeezed in more <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">meetings</span> while driving home. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> then ate dinner with her partner (who also works) and their children. Afterwards, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she<sup>A</sup></span> logged on to continue working until 1am, before falling asleep for three hours, getting up and doing it all over again. | Trên đường lái xe về nhà, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> vẫn tranh thủ <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">họp</span>. Về đến nơi, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> ăn tối với chồng (cũng đi làm) và các con, sau đó lại đăng nhập để tiếp tục làm việc đến 1 giờ sáng, rồi ngủ ba tiếng, dậy lặp lại chu trình cũ. |
| "There were points where <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> would not <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">sleep</span>, or I would do 48 hours in a row and then crash for seven hours," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash said</span>. | “Có lúc <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">tôi<sup>A</sup></span> hoàn toàn không <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">ngủ</span>, hoặc làm liên tục 48 tiếng rồi ngủ bù 7 tiếng,” <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga nói</span>. |
| There was always an excuse at the ready to avoid <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">meeting clashes</span>. | Luôn có sẵn lý do để tránh trùng <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">lịch họp</span>: |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> would blame <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">kids</span>, sick, 'Sorry, no internet', doctor's appointment." | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> thường đổ cho <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">con cái</span>, nói mình bị bệnh, ‘xin lỗi mất mạng’, hoặc có hẹn bác sĩ.” |
|  | Âm thầm nhân đôi, nhân ba thu nhập |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash</span> said she was switching tasks about every 20 minutes. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga</span> cho biết cô thay đổi từ việc này sang việc khác khoảng 20 phút một lần. |
| "A lot of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the tech [jobs]</span> are ticket-based, so if <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> are fast, you have spare time to do other things. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">It's<sup>A</sup></span> not like a <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">customer service role</span> where <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you're<sup>E</sup></span> constantly talking," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she said<sup>A</sup></span>. | “Nhiều việc trong <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">ngành công nghệ</span> theo dạng giải quyết 'ticket', nếu <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">bạn<sup>E</sup></span> nhanh nhẹn thì sẽ có thời gian trống để làm việc khác. Không giống như phục vụ khách hàng, lúc nào cũng phải nói chuyện,” <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô giải thích<sup>A</sup></span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> just had to learn to be faster and more efficient and utilise different tools, so with the introduction of AI, that helped a lot." | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> phải học cách làm việc nhanh hơn, hiệu quả hơn, tận dụng công cụ. Nhờ có AI, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">việc này<sup>A</sup></span> dễ hơn rất nhiều.” |
| It can be a lucrative business — <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash says</span> she raked in $500,000 during her busiest year. | <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Công việc này<sup>A</sup></span> mang lại lợi nhuận khổng lồ, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga nói</span> cô từng kiếm tới 500.000 đô la trong năm bận rộn nhất. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The Feed</span> has seen a bank statement and payment invoice from <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this time<sup>A</sup></span>, pointing to seven different income sources over a fortnight. | The Feed đã xem sao kê ngân hàng và hóa đơn thanh toán, cho thấy cô có tới bảy nguồn thu nhập khác nhau chỉ trong hai tuần. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash</span>'s schedule might seem extreme to some, but she said her aim was to support her family and avoid worrying about bills. | Dù lịch trình có vẻ khắc nghiệt, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga nói</span> mục tiêu của cô là nuôi gia đình và không phải lo <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hóa đơn</span>. |
| <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> is now down to working <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">two full-time jobs</span>. "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> don't see myself <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">doing this<sup>A</sup></span> forever, what I want to do is probably just get through <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the kids</span> through school." | Hiện tại, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> đã giảm xuống còn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hai công việc toàn thời gian</span>. “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> không nghĩ sẽ <span style="background-color:#00FF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Substitution (S) · LC">làm như vậy</span> mãi, cái tôi muốn là lo cho <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">con</span> học xong.” |
| <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">She<sup>A</sup></span> isn't alone. <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash</span> is part of a growing international community of workers hoping to multiply <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">their incomes</span> by flying under the radar. No matter the risks. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Nhưng</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga</span> không đơn độc. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Cô<sup>A</sup></span> là một phần trong cộng đồng quốc tế đang <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">âm thầm nhân đôi, nhân ba</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">thu nhập</span> bằng cách giấu kín <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">chuyện này<sup>A</sup></span> với <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">chủ lao động</span>, bất chấp rủi ro. |
| 'Work multiple jobs, reach financial freedom' | “Làm nhiều việc để đạt tự do tài chính” |
| More than half a million people have joined an online Reddit forum dedicated to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the practice</span> of secretly holding <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple full-time jobs</span>. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Its<sup>A</sup></span> tagline reads: "Work multiple jobs, reach financial freedom". | Hơn nửa triệu người đã tham gia diễn đàn Reddit chuyên bàn về việc bí mật giữ <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhiều công việc toàn thời gian</span>. Khẩu hiệu của <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">diễn đàn</span> là: “Work multiple jobs, reach financial freedom” (Làm nhiều việc, đạt tự do tài chính). |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Members</span> of <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this community<sup>A</sup></span> label themselves '<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployed</span>'. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">It's<sup>A</sup></span> a contemporary spin on 'moonlighting', with remote working now allowing people to take on <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">roles</span> simultaneously, rather than <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">after hours</span>. | Các <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">thành viên</span> tự gọi mình là “<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployed</span>”. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Đây<sup>A</sup></span> là biến tấu hiện đại của “moonlighting” (làm thêm giờ), khi <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm việc từ xa</span> cho phép họ <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm nhiều việc</span> cùng lúc thay vì sau giờ làm. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Members</span> — including <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Australians</span> — swap career advice and tips to avoid getting caught. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">They<sup>A</sup></span> <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">also</span> share experiences of burnout and getting sacked after their <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employers</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">caught on</span>. | Nhiều <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người Úc</span> chia sẻ lời khuyên nghề nghiệp, mẹo tránh bị phát hiện, kinh nghiệm bị kiệt sức và cả chuyện bị sa thải khi <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">chủ lao động</span> phát hiện. |
| <span style="background-color:#00FF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Substitution (S) · LC">Those</span> who spoke to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The Feed</span> stressed they wanted their identities hidden, to avoid potential <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">blowback</span> from <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employers</span> past and present. | Những người trò chuyện với <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The Feed</span> đều muốn giấu danh tính để tránh bị <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">chủ cũ</span> hay chủ hiện tại trừng phạt. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The threat</span> of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">retaliation</span> from <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employers</span> is all too real. Last month, software engineer Soham Parekh was publicly <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">exposed</span> on X by several Silicon Valley chief executives, who alleged they had <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">fired</span> him for working at multiple tech startups. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nguy cơ</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bị lộ</span> là rất thật. Tháng trước, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">kỹ sư phần mềm</span> Soham Parekh bị nhiều CEO ở Silicon Valley công khai tố cáo trên mạng X rằng ông ta <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bị sa thải</span> vì cùng lúc làm việc ở nhiều <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">startup công nghệ</span>. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">One Australian</span>, "Jess", said they were an engineer who had previously doubled their <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">income</span> by juggling <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">four full-time jobs</span>. For <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">them<sup>A</sup></span>, it was about having <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">extra money</span>, and what they saw as rebelling against the system. | Một <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người Úc</span> <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">khác<sup>A</sup></span>, “Tài” (tên đã thay đổi), từng là kỹ sư, cho biết họ đã từng <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân đôi thu nhập</span> bằng cách <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">xoay sở</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bốn công việc toàn thời gian</span>, để có thêm tiền và cũng là hành động phản kháng hệ thống. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> always made sure that I was producing good work. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">This<sup>A</sup></span> is not a case of slacking off and just riding the wave until <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> get <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">caught and fired</span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">they told<sup>A</sup></span> The Feed. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> luôn chắc rằng mình làm việc chất lượng. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Đây<sup>A</sup></span> không phải là chuyện lười biếng rồi hưởng lương đến khi <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bị bắt</span> và <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">sa thải</span>,” <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài nói</span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The CEOs</span> all have <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple jobs</span>, multiple things that they're running all at the same time … why should <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">we<sup>E</sup></span> just follow <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the rules</span> that the CEOs have decided?" | “<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Các CEO</span> đều có <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhiều công việc</span>, nhiều dự án cùng lúc … vậy tại sao <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">chúng ta<sup>E</sup></span> phải răm rắp theo luật mà CEO đặt ra?” |
| Emeritus professor Mark Wooden, a leading expert in labour markets at the University of Melbourne, said most <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employers</span> still expect <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">full-time employees</span> to work exclusively for <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">one company</span>. | Giáo sư Mark Wooden, chuyên gia hàng đầu về thị trường lao động tại Đại học Melbourne, cho rằng hầu hết <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">chủ lao động</span> vẫn mong <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên toàn thời gian</span> chỉ làm cho <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">một công ty</span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">They're<sup>A</sup></span> very nervous about <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employees</span> doing <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">other jobs</span> because … they would think that would normally mean productivity on their <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">first job</span> declines," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">he said<sup>A</sup></span>. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Họ<sup>A</sup></span> rất lo nếu <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên</span> làm thêm việc khác, vì nghĩ năng suất ở <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công việc chính</span> sẽ giảm,” <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">ông nói<sup>A</sup></span>. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess</span> believes traditional corporate ideals, such as loyalty to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a single company</span>, are outdated. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài</span> tin rằng những lý tưởng cũ như <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">sự trung thành với một công ty</span> đã lỗi thời. |
| "The majority of people who work on <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">white-collar jobs</span> in an office spend most of their time doing useless tasks, like going to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">meetings</span>," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess said</span>. | “Đa số <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên</span> văn phòng dành phần lớn thời gian làm những việc vô bổ như <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">đi họp</span>,” <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài nói</span>. |
| "If … <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> can get <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the work</span> done in less time than <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the 40 hours</span> and still keep the same level of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">productivity</span>, then <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">we<sup>E</sup></span> as free agents in the market should be able to <span style="background-color:#00FF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Substitution (S) · TC">do so</span>." | “Nếu <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">bạn<sup>E</sup></span> có thể <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hoàn thành công việc</span> trong ít hơn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">40 giờ</span> mà vẫn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hiệu quả</span> như cũ, thì <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">chúng ta<sup>E</sup></span> nên có quyền <span style="background-color:#00FF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Substitution (S) · TC">làm vậy</span> như những tác nhân tự do trên thị trường.” |
| <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">However</span>, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess</span> admits <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multi-tasking</span> can sometimes be difficult. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Tuy nhiên</span>, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài</span> cũng thừa nhận <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm nhiều việc một lúc</span> không dễ: |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> have done <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the two meetings</span> at the same time before though … <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you've<sup>E</sup></span> got one earpiece in just to listen, [then] the other one … It's insane, I wouldn't recommend it at all." | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> từng tham gia <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hai cuộc họp</span> cùng lúc… một tai nghe để nghe, tai kia để nói. Điên rồ lắm.” |
| 'Working three jobs to make ends meet' | “Làm ba việc để đủ sống” |
| While <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">juggling full-time office jobs</span> may seem unusual, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">having multiple jobs</span> is more common for part-time, casual, seasonal and gig workers in many industries, such as administrative services, agriculture and food services. | Trong khi việc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">xoay sở</span> nhiều công việc văn phòng toàn thời gian nghe có vẻ lạ, thì có nhiều ngành nghề phổ biến chuyện <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm nhiều việc</span>: bán thời gian, hợp đồng, mùa vụ hay gig (dịch vụ tự do) và dịch vụ ăn uống. |
| In March, 6.5 per cent of employed people had <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple jobs</span>, according to the Australian Bureau of Statistics . <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">This rate<sup>A</sup></span> has remained at an all-time high since December 2022, though it's a small increase over three decades (less than 2 per cent). | Theo Nha Thống kê Úc, vào tháng 3, 6,5% người có việc làm đang <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm nhiều công việc</span>, mức cao nhất kể từ cuối năm 2022. |
| <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Meanwhile</span>, Australia's unemployment rate was 4.2 per cent in July. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Trong khi đó</span>, tỷ lệ thất nghiệp ở Úc tháng 7 là 4,2%. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The Reddit community</span>'s use of '<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployment</span>' differs to the definition traditionally used by economists. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Tuy nhiên</span>, khái niệm “<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployment</span>” của <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">cộng đồng Reddit</span> khác so với cách hiểu của các nhà kinh tế. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Wooden said</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployment</span> usually refers to "anybody who's <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">working more hours</span> than they would prefer, after taking into account their income". | Theo <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giáo sư Wooden</span>, “<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployment</span>” truyền thống nghĩa là làm <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhiều giờ</span> hơn mong muốn so với nhu cầu <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">thu nhập</span>. |
| "It's not really about multiple job-holding per se, but <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple job-holders</span> could well be people in <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this category<sup>A</sup></span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">he said<sup>A</sup></span>. |  |
| Nearly a quarter of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple job-holders</span> would prefer to work <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">fewer hours</span> (i.e. they are <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployed</span>), compared to around 23 per cent of single job-holders, according to the 2023 Household, Income and Labour Dynamics in Australia (HILDA) survey. | Theo khảo sát HILDA 2023, gần 25% <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người làm nhiều việc</span> muốn giảm <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giờ làm</span>, so với khoảng 23% người chỉ làm một việc. |
| <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">However</span>, around 18 per cent of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple job-holders</span> would prefer to work even *more* hours, compared to around 14 per cent of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">single job-holders</span> – suggesting <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">people with multiple jobs</span> are more likely to be dissatisfied with their <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">working hours</span> either way. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Nhưng</span> 18% <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người làm nhiều việc</span> lại muốn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm thêm</span>, cao hơn mức 14% của <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người làm một việc</span> cho thấy cả hai nhóm đều có mức bất mãn với <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">số giờ làm</span>. |
| "Adam", who earns a $140,000 <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">salary package</span> at his single job, is looking to pick up <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a second full-time job</span>. | Một người đàn ông tên “Thanh”, với <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">gói lương</span> 140.000 đô, đang tìm thêm <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công việc toàn thời gian</span> thứ hai. |
| <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">He<sup>A</sup></span> hopes <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this<sup>A</sup></span> will be his ticket to owning a home in Sydney, where the median house price has jumped over $1.7 million, according to the latest Domain house price report. | <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Anh<sup>A</sup></span> hy vọng <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">đó<sup>A</sup></span> sẽ là tấm vé để sở hữu nhà ở Sydney, nơi giá nhà trung bình vượt 1,7 triệu đô. |
| "I just don't see any other way, other than working harder and <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">earning more</span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">he said<sup>A</sup></span>. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> không thấy cách nào khác ngoài việc làm nhiều hơn và <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">kiếm nhiều hơn</span>,” <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">anh nói<sup>A</sup></span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">It's<sup>A</sup></span> kind of just a reaction to being able to stay middle class." |  |

4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">her family</span> wouldn't be able to survive if she and <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" nhà.” |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">That's<sup>A</sup></span> weird that <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> feel grateful that I can have <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple jobs</span>, even though that's like American style, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">working three jobs to make ends meet</span>," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she said<sup>A</sup></span>. |  |

| The health impacts of working long hours | Tác động sức khỏe của làm việc nhiều giờ |
| The so-called '<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployed</span>' <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">community</span> is bucking a long-term work trend, amid growing calls for Australia to adopt a four-day work week. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Cộng đồng</span> “overemployed” đang đi ngược xu hướng giảm <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giờ làm</span>, trong khi nhiều người kêu gọi Úc áp dụng tuần làm bốn ngày. |
| Economist <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Mark Wooden</span> said there's been <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a gradual decline</span> in the proportion of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employed people</span> working <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">very long weeks</span> over the last quarter of a century. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">He said<sup>A</sup></span> in June 2025, just over 10 per cent of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employed people</span> reported usually working <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">50 or more hours</span> per week. In 2001, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">it<sup>A</sup></span> was more than 18 per cent. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Giáo sư Wooden</span> cho biết tỷ lệ người làm việc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">50 giờ/tuần</span> trở lên đã <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giảm đáng kể</span> trong 25 năm qua: từ hơn 18% năm 2001 xuống chỉ hơn 10% năm 2025. |
| Associate professor Nicole Black, a health economist at Monash University, has analysed research on the health impacts of cost-of-living pressures. | Phó giáo sư Nicole Black, nhà kinh tế y tế tại Đại học Monash, phân tích rằng áp lực chi phí sinh hoạt khiến nhiều người phải <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm nhiều giờ</span> hoặc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhiều việc</span>, nhưng hệ quả sức khỏe là rõ ràng. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">We<sup>E</sup></span> think about work and <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">productivity</span> being important, and it is important to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">make ends meet</span>, but there's often a negative consequence in terms of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">our health</span> if we overspend time working," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she said<sup>A</sup></span>. "There's a lot of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">literature</span> linking <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">longer work hours</span> to poorer <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">health outcomes</span>. | “Nếu <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">bạn<sup>E</sup></span> dành nhiều thời gian để làm việc, bạn sẽ có ít thời gian để tập thể dục, nấu ăn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">lành mạnh</span>, chăm sóc bản thân. <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Và</span> nghiên cứu đã chỉ ra <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm việc nhiều giờ</span> gắn với <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">sức khỏe</span> kém hơn.” |
| "Things like exercise, that takes time, cooking <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">healthy meals</span>, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">we know<sup>E</sup></span> that takes time, so it's going to impact other aspects of <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">your life<sup>E</sup></span>. Just the very fact that <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you've<sup>E</sup></span> got <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">24 hours a day</span>, if you're spending more of that time working, you've got less time to spend <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">being healthy</span>." |  |
| Although <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash said</span> she still enjoys <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">her work</span>, she's starting to feel symptoms of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">burnout</span> — and she's not the only one affected. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga</span> cho biết dù cô vẫn thích <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công việc</span>, nhưng bắt đầu thấy dấu hiệu <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">kiệt sức</span>. |
| "One thing that really impacted <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">me<sup>A</sup></span> was one of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">my kids</span> telling me: 'All you do is work'," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">she said<sup>A</sup></span>. | Một trong những điều khiến <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">cô<sup>A</sup></span> suy nghĩ nhiều là lời <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">con</span> nói: “Mẹ chỉ biết làm việc thôi.” |
| "When <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> had <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the seven jobs</span> … I would just push them off to the side until the end of the month or something. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> don't do <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this<sup>A</sup></span> as much because it has impacted <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the kids</span>." |  |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the engineer</span> said after spending a year juggling up to <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">four jobs</span> at a time, they're now taking a break from working. "There was a lot of knock-on effects in <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">personal life</span>. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> guess the added stress wasn't ideal, and I realised that I put that on myself," <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">they said<sup>A</sup></span>. "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">It<sup>A</sup></span> kind of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">burnt</span> <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">me<sup>A</sup></span> out pretty badly and I'm not super keen to go back to it." | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài</span>, sau một năm <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">xoay sở bốn công việc</span>, <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">cũng</span> đã quyết định nghỉ ngơi: “Có quá nhiều tác động tiêu cực đến đời sống cá nhân, căng thẳng thêm không đáng. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> bị <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">kiệt sức</span> và không hứng thú quay lại lối sống <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">đó<sup>A</sup></span> nữa.” |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Wooden said</span> the effects of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">overemployment</span> on <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">mental health</span> can be just as serious as the effects of underemployment. | Theo <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">giáo sư Wooden</span>, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">tác động</span> tinh thần của <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">làm việc quá nhiều</span> cũng nghiêm trọng không kém tình trạng <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">thất nghiệp</span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">The difference</span> comes down to whether <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> do or you don't want to do <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">it<sup>A</sup></span>," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Wooden explained</span>. "The people who say, 'I'm working <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">75 hours a week</span> and I love it' … <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> might think they're mad, but they don't report their job satisfaction as bad, they don't report their <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">mental health</span> as bad. "<span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">But</span> if they say, 'I'm working <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">75 hours a week</span> and I wish I was only working 50' … <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">those people<sup>A</sup></span> are much less happy with <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">their jobs</span>, more likely to report <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">anxiety and symptoms of depression</span>." |  |
| Is it lawful to have multiple full-time jobs? | Làm nhiều công việc toàn thời gian có hợp pháp không? |
| Whether or not <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you're<sup>E</sup></span> allowed to have <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">multiple full-time jobs</span> depends on your individual employment contract, according to employment lawyer Danny King. | Theo luật sư lao động Danny King, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">điều này<sup>A</sup></span> phụ thuộc vào hợp đồng lao động cá nhân. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">You've<sup>E</sup></span> got to be careful that <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">it<sup>A</sup></span> doesn't have obligations of exclusivity. <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">And</span> in a lot of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">contract templates</span>, <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you'll<sup>E</sup></span> see that you can't have any <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">other<sup>A</sup></span> engagement, paid or otherwise," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">King said</span>. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Bạn<sup>E</sup></span> phải cẩn thận xem <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hợp đồng</span> có điều khoản độc quyền không. Nhiều <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">mẫu hợp đồng</span> ghi rõ <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên</span> không được làm thêm bất kỳ công việc nào <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">khác<sup>A</sup></span>, có lương hay không,” <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">bà nói<sup>A</sup></span>. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Employees</span> must <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">also</span> meet <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">their obligations</span> by avoiding underperforming in <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">their role</span>. |  |
| "If <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">get caught</span>, but you can demonstrate that you didn't lie and you have otherwise satisfied <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">your obligations</span> in <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the contract</span> to deliver <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">the work</span> that you're paid to do, it might be that you've got a case," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">King said</span>. | Nếu <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bị phát hiện</span> nhưng vẫn chứng minh mình không nói dối, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hoàn thành công việc</span> đúng như <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hợp đồng</span>, thì có thể vẫn được coi là <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hợp lệ</span>. |
| Breaches of <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">an employment contract</span> could be considered misconduct, which could lead to a warning, discussion or <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">termination of employment</span>. | <span style="background-color:#FF9900;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Conjunction (C) · LC">Nhưng</span> <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">vi phạm hợp đồng</span> có thể bị xem là sai phạm, dẫn đến cảnh cáo, kỷ luật hoặc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">sa thải</span>. |
| While it's technically possible for an <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employer</span> to take legal action against <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a worker</span> over loss of profit, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">King said</span> realistically, <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a case</span> would usually be too expensive and difficult for a business to pursue. | Dù lý thuyết thì <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công ty</span> có thể kiện <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">nhân viên</span> vì mất lợi nhuận, nhưng theo <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">bà King</span>, thực tế chi phí kiện tụng thường quá cao và khó để theo đuổi. |
| Both <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash</span> and <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess</span> claim they have never <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">breached their employment contracts</span>, nor simultaneously worked for <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a company</span>'s competitors. | <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Nga</span> và <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Tài</span> đều khẳng định họ chưa bao giờ làm cho đối thủ cạnh tranh hoặc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">vi phạm hợp đồng</span>. |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> didn't want <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">my employers</span> to feel was that there was time theft … so I did <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">insane hours</span>," <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Ash said</span>. | “<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> không muốn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">người chủ</span> nghĩ mình ăn cắp thời gian… nên mới làm việc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">điên cuồng</span>,” <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">chị Nga</span> nói. |
| <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">Jess said</span>: "If <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">you<sup>E</sup></span> can get <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">a company</span> where they are just like: 'We don't care what you do in your own time, as long as you <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">deliver</span> what we expect', then that's basically a green light to do it. | “Nếu <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">công ty</span> không quan tâm <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">bạn<sup>E</sup></span> làm gì ngoài giờ, miễn là bạn <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">hoàn thành</span> kỳ vọng, thì đó coi như đèn xanh. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">Tôi<sup>A</sup></span> không nghĩ có gì sai về đạo đức. Chỉ là nhiều người tỏ ra quá khắt khe thôi, còn <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">tôi<sup>A</sup></span> nghĩ đã đến lúc <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">các công ty</span> phải thay đổi.” |
| "<span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> don't think there's anything ethically wrong with that. <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">I<sup>A</sup></span> just think people get all up in arms and clutch their pearls about <span style="background-color:#FFFF00;color:#000000;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Reference (R) · LC">this sort of thing<sup>A</sup></span>, but I think <span style="background-color:#9900FF;color:#FFFFFF;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px" title="Lexical cohesion (L) · LC">employers</span> have had their day." |  |
